    for (var attr in obj2) { obj3[attr] = obj2[attr]; }
    return obj3;
  }
  if (typeof bcSfFilterConfig !== 'undefined') {
    var bcSfFilterConfig = mergeObject(bcSfFilterConfig, bcSfFilterMainConfig); 
  } else {
    var bcSfFilterConfig = mergeObject({}, bcSfFilterMainConfig); 
  }
</script>
